\widetilde{r}^{2}

as provided by Lemma
F.3
,
in particular using that
Q^{\pi^{M}}_{h}(s,a;p^{M},r)=Q^{\widehat{\pi}^{M}}_{h}(s,a;\widehat{p}^{M},\widehat{r})
,
at (4) we have applied triangle inequality, to bring the absolute value inside
the summation,
at (5) we upper bound

V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)\leq H\|r\|_{\infty}
and

V^{\widehat{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widehat{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\leq H\|\widehat{r}\|_{\infty}
,
and since
M\coloneqq 1/\max\{\|r\|_{\infty},\|\widehat{r}\|_{\infty}\}
, we obtain
H\|r\|_{\infty}/M\leq H

and
H\|\widehat{r}\|_{\infty}/M\leq H
; at (6) we have applied Pinsker’s inequality,
and at (7) we have applied the bound of
\mathcal{E}_{3}\supseteq\mathcal{E}

noticing that
N_{h}^{b}(s,a)\geq 1
because of event
\mathcal{E}_{1}\supseteq\mathcal{E}
.

A similar procedure can be carried out also for the supersets, using Lemma
F.4

instead of Lemma
F.3
.
∎

Finally, we have all the tools we need to prove Theorem
5.1
.
See
5.1

Proof.

First, observe that, thanks to Lemma
F.1
and Lemma
F.2
, we have that
good event
\mathcal{E}
holds w.p.
1-\delta

with a number of trajectories
\tau^{E}
and
\tau^{b}
upper bounded as in events
\mathcal{E}_{1}
and
\mathcal{E}_{2}
.
Now, under good event
\mathcal{E}
,
the idea of the proof is to compute the number of trajectories
\tau^{b}
needed to
have a distance between sets of rewards smaller than
\epsilon
.
Then, we combine it with the number of trajectories required by event
\mathcal{E}

through
\max\{a,b\}\leq a+b
for
a,b\geq 0
.

Let us begin with the subset. Thanks to Lemma
F.5
,
we can write:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{d}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap},\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap})

\displaystyle\leq H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(\cdot,\cdot)}b_{h}(s,a)

\displaystyle=H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\sum\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(s,a)b_{h}(s,a)

\displaystyle=H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\sum\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(s,a)\sqrt{2\frac{\beta(N^{b}_{h}(s,a),\delta)}{N^{b}_{h}(s,a)}}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{\leq}\sqrt{2}H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\sum\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(s,a)\sqrt{\frac{\beta(\tau^{b},\delta)}{N^{b}_{h}(s,a)}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{2\beta(\tau^{b},\delta)}H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\sum\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(s,a)\sqrt{\frac{1}{N^{b}_{h}(s,a)}}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}\sqrt{2\beta(\tau^{b},\delta)}H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\sum\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(s,a)\sqrt{c_{4}\frac{\ln\frac{|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}{\delta}}{\tau^{b}\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(s,a)}}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{=}c_{5}\sqrt{\frac{\beta(\tau^{b},\delta)\ln\frac{|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}{\delta}}{\tau^{b}}}H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\sum\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}\sqrt{\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(s,a)}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(4)}}}{\leq}c_{5}\sqrt{\frac{\beta(\tau^{b},\delta)\ln\frac{|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}{\delta}}{\tau^{b}}}H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\sqrt{|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}|}\sqrt{\sum_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(s,a)}

\displaystyle=c_{5}\sqrt{\frac{\beta(\tau^{b},\delta)\ln\frac{|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}{\delta}}{\tau^{b}}}H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\sqrt{|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}|}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(5)}}}{\leq}c_{5}\sqrt{\frac{\beta(\tau^{b},\delta)\ln\frac{|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}{\delta}}{\tau^{b}}}H\sqrt{H|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}\leq\epsilon,

where at (1) we have used that
\tau^{b}\geq N^{b}_{h}(s,a)
for all
(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}
, and that function
\beta(\cdot,\delta)

is monotonically increasing; at (2) we have applied the result in Lemma
F.2
for
event
\mathcal{E}_{4}
, at (3) we define constant
c_{5}\coloneqq\sqrt{2c_{4}}
, and at (4) and (5)
we have applied the Cauchy-Schwarz’s inequality.

To compute an upper bound to the number of trajectories required to have

\mathcal{H}_{d}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap},\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap})\leq\epsilon
,
we compute the smallest
\tau^{b}
that satisfies:

\displaystyle c_{5}\sqrt{\frac{\beta(\tau^{b},\delta)\ln\frac{|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}{\delta}}{\tau^{b}}}H\sqrt{H|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}\leq\epsilon.

By using the definition of
\beta(\tau^{b},\delta)

from Eq.
17
and rearranging the terms, this is equivalent
to finding the smallest
\tau^{b}
such that:

\displaystyle\tau^{b}\geq c_{6}\frac{H^{3}|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|\ln\frac{|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}{\delta}\bigl(\ln\frac{4|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}{\delta}+(|\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{\max}|-1)\ln\bigl(e(1+\tau^{b}/(|\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{\max}|-1))\bigr)\bigr)}{\epsilon^{2}},

where
c_{6}\coloneqq c_{5}^{2}
.
If we define:

\displaystyle a

\displaystyle\coloneqq c_{6}\frac{H^{3}|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|\ln\frac{|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}{\delta}\ln\frac{4|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}{\delta}}{\epsilon^{2}},

\displaystyle b

\displaystyle\coloneqq c_{6}\frac{H^{3}|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|\ln\frac{|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}{\delta}(|\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{\max}|-1)}{\epsilon^{2}},

\displaystyle c

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{e}{|\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{\max}|-1},

\displaystyle d

\displaystyle\coloneqq e,

then we can rewrite the previous expression as:

\displaystyle\tau^{b}\geq a+b\ln(c\tau^{b}+d).

To solve it, we can notice that

a,b,c,d>0
and
2bc>e
, thus we can apply Lemma
J.3
17
17

17

It should be remarked that the adoption of Lemma 15 of
(
Kaufmann et al. 2021
)

provides the same asymptotical dependence on the quantities of interest.
However, Lemma
J.3
is more concise.

to obtain:

\displaystyle\tau^{b}\geq 2a+3b\ln(2bc)+d/c.

Replacing
a,b,c,d
with their values, we get:

\displaystyle\tau^{b}

\displaystyle\geq 2c_{6}\frac{H^{3}|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|\ln\frac{|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}{\delta}\ln\frac{4|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}{\delta}}{\epsilon^{2}}+\bigl(|\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{\max}|-1\bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad+3c_{6}\frac{H^{3}|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|\ln\frac{|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}{\delta}(|\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{\max}|-1)}{\epsilon^{2}}\ln\biggl(2c_{6}e\frac{H^{3}|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|\ln\frac{|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}{\delta}}{\epsilon^{2}}\biggr)

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\Biggl(\frac{H^{3}|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|\ln\frac{1}{\delta}}{\epsilon^{2}}\biggl(\ln\frac{1}{\delta}+|\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{\max}|\biggr)\Biggr).

Now, observe that an upper bound to the number of trajectories needed to have

\mathcal{H}_{d}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup},\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup})\leq\epsilon

can be obtained with an identical derivation, and, thus, it is of the same order.
The statement of the theorem follows by the considerations at the beginning of the proof.
∎

We provide here the proof of Theorem
5.2
.
The proof is analogous to that of Theorem
5.1
.
See
5.2

Sketch of proof.

The proof is exactly the same as that of Theorem
5.1
.
First, observe that it is possible to prove a lemma analogous to Lemma
F.5
, so that:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\infty}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap},\widehat{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap})

\displaystyle\leq H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\max\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}b_{h}(s,a).

Next, when applying Lemma
J.1
, we simply notice that, for all
h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket
:

\displaystyle\max\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(s,a)}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{\rho^{\pi^{b},\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}_{\min}}}.

We can do the same also for the superset. Following the derivation in the proof of Theorem
5.1
, the result can be obtained.
∎

F.3
Proof of Theorem
6.1
and Theorem
6.2

We will denote by
a^{E}\coloneqq\pi^{E}_{h}(s)
for all
(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}
, the expert’s action.
Given any reward
r\in\mathfrak{R}
, it is useful to define (recursively) the transition models
\widetilde{p}^{M}
and
\widetilde{p}^{m}
as:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\widetilde{p}^{M}&\coloneqq\begin{cases}\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)=\argmax\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}p^{\prime}:\|p^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)-\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)\|_{1}\leq b_{h}(s,a)\\
\wedge\forall s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}:\,p^{\prime}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)=0\end{subarray}}\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}^{\prime}(\cdot|s,a)}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},r)\},\;\text{if }(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}\\
\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)=\mathbbm{1}\{\cdot=\argmax\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},r)\},\;\text{if }(s,a,h)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}\\
\end{cases},\\
\widetilde{p}^{m}&\coloneqq\begin{cases}\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)=\argmin\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}p^{\prime}:\|p^{\prime}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)-\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)\|_{1}\leq b_{h}(s,a)\\
\wedge\forall s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}:\,p^{\prime}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)=0\end{subarray}}\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}^{\prime}(\cdot|s,a)}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},r)\},\;\text{if }(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}\\
\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)=\mathbbm{1}\{\cdot=\argmin\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},r)\},\;\text{if }(s,a,h)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}\\
\end{cases},\end{split}

(25)

where we have used the following (recursive) policy definitions
\widetilde{\pi}^{M},\widetilde{\pi}^{m}\in[\pi^{E}]_{\equiv_{\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}}
:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\widetilde{\pi}^{M}&\coloneqq\begin{cases}\widetilde{\pi}^{M}_{h}(s)=\pi^{E}_{h}(s),\;\text{if }(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}\\
\widetilde{\pi}^{M}_{h}(\cdot|s)=\mathbbm{1}\{\cdot=\argmax\limits_{a\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h}(s,a;\widetilde{p}^{M},r)\},\;\text{if }(s,h)\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}\\
\end{cases},\\
\widetilde{\pi}^{m}&\coloneqq\begin{cases}\widetilde{\pi}^{m}_{h}(s)=\pi^{E}_{h}(s),\;\text{if }(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}\\
\widetilde{\pi}^{m}_{h}(\cdot|s)=\mathbbm{1}\{\cdot={\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\argmax\limits_{a\in\mathcal{A}}}Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h}(s,a;\widetilde{p}^{m},r)\},\;\text{if }(s,h)\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}\end{cases}.\end{split}

(26)

Thanks to these definitions, we can rewrite
\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}
and
\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}
as:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}&=\{r\in\mathfrak{R}\,|\,\forall(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}},\forall a\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\{a^{E}\}:Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\widetilde{p}^{m}},r)\geq Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h}(s,a;{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\widetilde{p}^{M}},r)\},\\
\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}&=\{r\in\mathfrak{R}\,|\,\forall(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}},\forall a\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\{a^{E}\}:Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\widetilde{p}^{M}},r)\geq Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h}(s,a;{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\widetilde{p}^{m}},r)\}.\end{split}

(27)

Both Theorem
6.1
and
Theorem
6.2
uses
d_{\infty}
instead of
d

use the same reward choice lemmas, but differ for the performance decomposition lemmas.

Lemma F.6

(Reward Choice Subset)
.

For any
r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}
,
the reward
\widehat{r}
constructed as:

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a^{E})=r_{h}(s,a^{E})+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r)-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r}),\quad\forall(s,a^{E},h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}\\
\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)=r_{h}(s,a)+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p^{M}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r}),\quad\text{otherwise},\end{cases},

belongs to
\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}
.

Proof.

Consider any
(s,a^{E},h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}
.
By definition of
\widehat{r}
, by rearranging the terms, we have that:

\displaystyle\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a^{E})+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})=r_{h}(s,a^{E})+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r)

\displaystyle\iff Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})=Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r).

(28)

Now, consider any other triple
(s,a,h)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}
. Similarly, by rearranging the terms,
we obtain:

\displaystyle Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h}(s,a;\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})=Q^{\pi^{M}}_{h}(s,a;p^{M},r).

(29)

By hypothesis,
r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}
, therefore:

\displaystyle\forall(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}},\forall a\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\{a^{E}\}:Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r)\geq Q^{\pi^{M}}_{h}(s,a;p^{M},r),

from which it follows that:

\displaystyle\forall(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}},\forall a\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\{a^{E}\}:Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\geq Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h}(s,a;\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r}).

∎

For the superset, we have an analogous result.

Lemma F.7

(Reward Choice Superset)
.

For any
\widehat{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}
,
the reward
r
constructed as:

\displaystyle\begin{cases}r_{h}(s,a^{E})=\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a^{E})+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r),\quad\forall(s,a^{E},h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}\\
r_{h}(s,a)=\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p^{m}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r),\quad\text{otherwise},\end{cases},

belongs to
\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}
.

Proof.

Consider any
(s,a^{E},h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}
.
By definition of
r
, by rearranging the terms, we have that:

\displaystyle\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a^{E})+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})=r_{h}(s,a^{E})+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r)

\displaystyle\iff Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})=Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r).

(30)

Now, consider any other triple
(s,a,h)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}
. Similarly, by rearranging the terms,
we obtain:

\displaystyle Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h}(s,a;\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})=Q^{\pi^{m}}_{h}(s,a;p^{m},r).

(31)

By hypothesis,
\widehat{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}
, therefore:

\displaystyle\forall(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}},\forall a\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\{a^{E}\}:Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\geq Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h}(s,a;\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r}),

from which it follows that:

\displaystyle\forall(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}},\forall a\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\{a^{E}\}:Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r)\geq Q^{\pi^{m}}_{h}(s,a;p^{m},r).

Since
p^{m}\in[p]_{\equiv_{\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}}
and
\pi^{m}
is the worst policy in
[\pi^{E}]_{\equiv_{\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}}
for
p^{m}
, then we have
r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}
.
∎

F.3.1
Lemmas for Theorem
6.1

Lemma F.8

(Performance Decomposition Subset)
.

Under good event
\mathcal{E}
, it holds that:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{d}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap},\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap})

\displaystyle\leq 2H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(\cdot,\cdot)}b_{h}(s,a)+8H^{3}\max\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h}(s,a).

Proof.

Observe that:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{d}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap},\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap})

\displaystyle\coloneqq\max\{\sup\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}}\inf\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}}d(r,\widetilde{r}),\sup\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}}\inf\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}}d(r,\widetilde{r})\}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\sup\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}}\inf\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}}d(r,\widetilde{r})

\displaystyle\eqqcolon\sup\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}}\inf\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}}\frac{1}{M}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\Bigl(\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(\cdot,\cdot)}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widetilde{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|+\max\limits_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widetilde{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|\Bigr)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}\sup\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}}\frac{1}{M}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\Bigl(\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(\cdot,\cdot)}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|+\max\limits_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|\Bigr),

(32)

where at (1) we have used that, under event
\mathcal{E}
, we have
\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}\subseteq\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}
,
and at (2) we have applied Lemma
F.6
, denoting with
\widehat{r}
the reward chosen from
\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}
.

Now, we consider the various triples
(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\llbracket H\rrbracket
differently according to the definition
of
\widehat{r}
in Lemma
F.6
.
Let us begin with any
(s,a^{E},h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}
.
Thanks to Eq.
F.3
,
we know that, for any
(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}
, it holds that

Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r)=Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})
.
Since any expert’s action when played from
\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}
brings to

\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}
(even under
\widetilde{p}^{m}
, by definition)
, then we can write:

\displaystyle\bigl|r_{h}(s,a^{E})-\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a^{E})\bigr|

\displaystyle=\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r)-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle=\bigl|\sum\limits_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r)-\sum\limits_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}}\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r)-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}p,r})\bigr|

\displaystyle=\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})-\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E}))V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r)\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\bigl|(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})-\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E}))HM\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq HM\bigl\|p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})-\hat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})\bigr\|_{1}+HM\bigl\|\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})-\hat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})\bigr\|_{1}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{\leq}2MHb_{h}(s,a^{E}),

(33)

where at (1) we use Eq.
F.3
,
at (2) we use triangle inequality and we upper bound the
value function by
H
times the maximum reward,
and at (3) we use the event in Lemma

F.2
twice.

Now, let us consider any triple
(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}\setminus\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}
.
Thanks to Lemma
F.6
, we can write:

\displaystyle\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|

\displaystyle=\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p^{M}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}p_{h}}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\pm\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)-\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a))V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\bigl(V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)-V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr)\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{\leq}2MHb_{h}(s,a)+\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\bigl(V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)-V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr)\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(4)}}}{\leq}2MHb_{h}(s,a)+\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\bigl(Q^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pi^{E}}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}p},r)-Q^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pi^{E}}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr)\bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\bigl(\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};p^{M},r)-\max\limits_{a^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr)\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(5)}}}{\leq}2MHb_{h}(s,a)+\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\bigl(Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r)-Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr)\bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\bigl(\underbrace{\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};p^{M},r)-\max\limits_{a^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pi^{M}}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime\prime};{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}p^{M},r})}_{=0}\bigr)\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq 2MHb_{h}(s,a)+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\underbrace{\bigl|Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r)-Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr|}_{\eqqcolon X_{h+1}(s^{\prime})},

where at (1) we have used that, since
(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}
, then
p^{M}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)=p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)
,
at (2) we have applied triangle inequality, at (3) we upper bound the
value function by
H
times the maximum reward and we use the event in Lemma

F.2
twice, at (4) we use triangle inequality and the Bellman’s equation and that the expert’s action
a^{E}
is played by both
\pi^{M}
and
\widetilde{\pi}^{M}
in any
(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}
,
at (5) we apply Eq.
29
.

Now, having defined terms
X_{h}(s)
for all
(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}
as above, we recursively bound term
X_{h+1}(s^{\prime})
.

\displaystyle X_{h+1}(s^{\prime})

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigl|Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r)-Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(6)}}}{=}\bigl|r_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E})-\widehat{r}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E})\bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+2}}p_{h+1}(s^{\prime\prime}|s^{\prime},a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};p,r)-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+2}}\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h+1}(s^{\prime\prime}|s^{\prime},a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr)\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(7)}}}{\leq}2MHb_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E})+\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+2}}p_{h+1}(s^{\prime\prime}|s^{\prime},a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};p,r)-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+2}}\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h+1}(s^{\prime\prime}|s^{\prime},a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad\pm\sum\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+2}}p_{h+1}(s^{\prime\prime}|s^{\prime},a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(8)}}}{\leq}2MHb_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E})+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+2}}\bigl|\bigl(p_{h+1}(s^{\prime\prime}|s^{\prime},a^{E})-\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h+1}(s^{\prime\prime}|s^{\prime},a^{E})\bigr)V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+2}}p_{h+1}(s^{\prime\prime}|s^{\prime},a^{E})\bigl|V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};p,r)-V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(9)}}}{\leq}4MHb_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E})+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+2}}p_{h+1}(s^{\prime\prime}|s^{\prime},a^{E})\underbrace{\bigl|Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime},a^{E};p,r)-Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr|}_{\eqqcolon X_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime})},

where at (6) we use the definition of Q function, and we apply triangle inequality;
at (7) we use again triangle inequality and Eq.
F.3.1
,
at (8) we apply triangle inequality, and at (9) we use again the event in Lemma
F.2
twice.
The recursion on the
X
terms tells us that:

\displaystyle X_{h}(s)\leq 4MHb_{h}(s,a^{E})+\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})}X_{h+1}(s^{\prime}).

(34)

Therefore,
we can upper bound the difference between rewards in
(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}\setminus\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}
as:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|&\leq 2MHb_{h}(s,a)+4MH\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}\in\llbracket h+1,H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\rho_{h^{\prime}}^{p,\pi^{E}}(\cdot|s_{h+1}=s^{\prime})}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime\prime},a^{E}).\end{split}

(35)

Now, the only missing triples to consider are those
(s,a,h)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}
.
Similarly to the triples just considered, we can write:

\displaystyle\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|

\displaystyle=\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p^{M}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\bigl|\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)-\max\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\bigl|V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)-V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{=}\max\Bigl\{\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\bigl|V^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pi^{E}}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}p},r)-V^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pi^{E}}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr|,

\displaystyle\qquad\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\bigl|\underbrace{V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)-V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})}_{=0}\bigr|\Bigr\}

\displaystyle=\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\bigl|Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r)-Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\eqqcolon\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}X_{h+1}(s^{\prime})

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(4)}}}{\leq}4MH\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\Bigl(b_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E})+\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}\in\llbracket h+2,H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\rho^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h+1}=s^{\prime})}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime\prime},a^{E})\Bigr),

(36)

where at (1) we have used that
(s,a,h)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}
and the definitions of
p^{M}

and
\widetilde{p}^{M}
, at (2) we have used that for any pair of real-valued functions
f,g

it holds that
|\max_{x}f(x)-\max_{x}g(x)|\leq\max_{x}|f(x)-g(x)|
,
at (3) we use Eq.
29
to realize that
in
(s,h)
outside
\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}
we have an equality of Q-functions, and thus the difference is 0,
at (4) we have unfolded the recursion on the
X
terms by using Eq.
34
.

By combining Eq.
F.3.1

with Eq.
F.3.1
,
Eq.
35
,
and Eq.
F.3.1
,
we get:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{d}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap},\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap})

\displaystyle\leq\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\biggl(\sum\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(s,a)2Hb_{h}(s,a)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}\setminus\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(s,a)\Bigl(2Hb_{h}(s,a)

\displaystyle+4H\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}\in\llbracket h+1,H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\rho_{h^{\prime}}^{p,\pi^{E}}(\cdot|s_{h+1}=s^{\prime})}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime\prime},a^{E})\Bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad+\max\limits_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}4H\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}\in\llbracket h+1,H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\rho^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h+1}=s^{\prime})}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime\prime},a^{E})\biggr)

\displaystyle\leq\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\biggl(\sum\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(s,a)2Hb_{h}(s,a)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}\setminus\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(s,a)\Bigl(2Hb_{h}(s,a)+4H^{2}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},a^{E},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E})\Bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad+\max\limits_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}4H^{2}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},a^{E},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E})\biggr)

\displaystyle\leq\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\biggl(\sum\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(s,a)2Hb_{h}(s,a)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}\setminus\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(s,a)2Hb_{h}(s,a)+8H^{2}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},a^{E},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E})\biggr)

\displaystyle\leq 2H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(\cdot,\cdot)}b_{h}(s,a)+8H^{3}\max\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h}(s,a).

∎

Lemma F.9

(Performance Decomposition Superset)
.

Under good event
\mathcal{E}
, it holds that:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{d}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup})

\displaystyle\leq 2H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(\cdot,\cdot)}b_{h}(s,a)+8H^{3}\max\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h}(s,a).

Proof.

Observe that:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{d}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup},\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup})

\displaystyle\coloneqq\max\{\sup\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}}\inf\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}}d(r,\widetilde{r}),\sup\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}}\inf\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}}d(r,\widetilde{r})\}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\sup\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}}\inf\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}}d(r,\widetilde{r})

\displaystyle\eqqcolon\sup\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}}\inf\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}}\frac{1}{M}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\Bigl(\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(\cdot,\cdot)}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widetilde{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|+\max\limits_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widetilde{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|\Bigr)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}\sup\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}}\frac{1}{M}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\Bigl(\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(\cdot,\cdot)}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widetilde{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|+\max\limits_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widetilde{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|\Bigr),

(37)

where at (1) we have used that, under event
\mathcal{E}
, we have
\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}\subseteq\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}
,
and at (2) we have applied Lemma
F.7
, denoting with
r
the reward chosen from
\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}
.

Now, we consider the various triples
(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\llbracket H\rrbracket
differently according to the definition
of
r
in Lemma
F.7
.
Let us begin with any
(s,a^{E},h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}
.
We can write:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a^{E})-\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a^{E})\bigr|&=\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r)-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\\
&=\bigl|\sum\limits_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r)-\sum\limits_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}}\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr|\\
&=\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r)-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}p,r})\bigr|\\
&\leq\bigl\|p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})-\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})\bigr\|_{1}MH\\
&\leq\bigl\|p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})-\hat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})\bigr\|_{1}MH+\bigl\|\hat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})-\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})\bigr\|_{1}MH\\
&\leq 2MHb_{h}(s,a^{E}).\end{split}

(38)

Now, let us consider any triple
(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}\setminus\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}
.
Thanks to Lemma
F.7
, we can write:

\displaystyle\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|

\displaystyle=\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p^{m}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r)-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)}V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r)-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})

\displaystyle\qquad\pm\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}(p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)-\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a))V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\bigl(V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r)-V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr)\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{\leq}2MHb_{h}(s,a)+\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\bigl(V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r)-V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr)\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(4)}}}{\leq}2MHb_{h}(s,a)+\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\bigl(Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r)-Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr)\bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\bigl(\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};p^{m},r)-\max\limits_{a^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr)\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(5)}}}{\leq}2MHb_{h}(s,a)+\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\bigl(Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r)-Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr)\bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\bigl(\underbrace{\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};p^{m},r)-\max\limits_{a^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime\prime};{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}p^{m},r})}_{=0}\bigr)\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq 2MHb_{h}(s,a)+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\underbrace{\bigl|Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r)-Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|}_{\eqqcolon Y_{h+1}(s^{\prime})},

where at (1) we have used that, since
(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}
, then
p^{m}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)=p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)
,
at (2) we have applied triangle inequality, at (3) we upper bound the
value function by
H
times the maximum reward and we use the event in Lemma

F.2
twice, at (4) we use triangle inequality and the Bellman optimality equation and that the expert’s action
a^{E}
is optimal in any
(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}
,
at (5) we apply Eq.
31
.

Now, having defined terms
\{Y_{h}(s)\}_{h}
for all
s\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}
as above, we recursively bound term
Y_{h+1}(s^{\prime})
. It should be remarked that

(s^{\prime},h+1)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}
.

\displaystyle Y_{h+1}(s^{\prime})

\displaystyle\coloneqq\bigl|Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r)-Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(6)}}}{=}\bigl|r_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E})-\widehat{r}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E})\bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+2}}p_{h+1}(s^{\prime\prime}|s^{\prime},a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};p,r)-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+2}}\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h+1}(s^{\prime\prime}|s^{\prime},a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr)\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(7)}}}{\leq}2MHb_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E})+\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+2}}p_{h+1}(s^{\prime\prime}|s^{\prime},a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};p,r)-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+2}}\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h+1}(s^{\prime\prime}|s^{\prime},a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad\pm\sum\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+2}}p_{h+1}(s^{\prime\prime}|s^{\prime},a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(8)}}}{\leq}2MHb_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E})+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+2}}\bigl|\bigl(p_{h+1}(s^{\prime\prime}|s^{\prime},a^{E})-\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h+1}(s^{\prime\prime}|s^{\prime},a^{E})\bigr)V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+2}}p_{h+1}(s^{\prime\prime}|s^{\prime},a^{E})\bigl|V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};p,r)-V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(9)}}}{\leq}4MHb_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E})+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+2}}p_{h+1}(s^{\prime\prime}|s^{\prime},a^{E})\underbrace{\bigl|Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime},a^{E};p,r)-Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|}_{\eqqcolon Y_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime})},

where at (6) we use the definition of Q function, and we apply triangle inequality;
at (7) we use again triangle inequality and Eq.
38
,
at (8) we apply triangle inequality, and at (9) we use again the event in Lemma
F.2
twice.
The recursion on the
Y
terms tells us that:

\displaystyle Y_{h}(s)\leq 4MHb_{h}(s,a^{E})+\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})}Y_{h+1}(s^{\prime}).

(39)

Therefore,
we can upper bound the difference between rewards in
(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}\setminus\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}
as:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|&\leq 2MHb_{h}(s,a)+4MH\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}\in\llbracket h+1,H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\rho_{h^{\prime}}^{p,\pi^{E}}(\cdot|s_{h+1}=s^{\prime})}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime\prime},a^{E}).\end{split}

(40)

Now, the only missing triples to consider are those
(s,a,h)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}
.
Similarly to the triples just considered, we can write:

\displaystyle\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|

\displaystyle=\bigl|\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p^{m}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r)-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\bigl|\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r)-\min\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\bigl|V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r)-V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{=}\max\Bigl\{\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\bigl|V^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pi^{E}}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}p},r)-V^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pi^{E}}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|,

\displaystyle\qquad\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\bigl|\underbrace{V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r)-V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})}_{=0}\bigr|\Bigr\}

\displaystyle=\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\bigl|Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r)-Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\eqqcolon\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}Y_{h+1}(s^{\prime})

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(4)}}}{\leq}4MH\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\Bigl(b_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E})+\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}\in\llbracket h+2,H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\rho^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h+1}=s^{\prime})}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime\prime},a^{E})\Bigr),

(41)

where at (1) we have used that
(s,a,h)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}
and the definitions of
p^{m}

and
\widetilde{p}^{m}
, at (2) we have used that for any pair of real-valued functions
f,g

it holds that
|\min_{x}f(x)-\min_{x}g(x)|\leq\max_{x}|f(x)-g(x)|
,
at (3) we use Eq.
31
to realize that
in
(s,h)
outside
\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}
we have an equality of Q-functions, and thus the difference is 0,
and that in
\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}
the optimal action is always the expert’s action,
at (4) we have unfolded the recursion on the
Y
terms by using Eq.
39
.

By combining Eq.
F.3.1

with Eq.
38
,
Eq.
40
,
and Eq.
F.3.1
,
we get:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{d}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup},\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup})

\displaystyle\leq\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\biggl(\sum\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(s,a)2Hb_{h}(s,a)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}\setminus\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(s,a)\Bigl(2Hb_{h}(s,a)

\displaystyle+4H\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}\in\llbracket h+1,H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\rho_{h^{\prime}}^{p,\pi^{E}}(\cdot|s_{h+1}=s^{\prime})}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime\prime},a^{E})\Bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad+\max\limits_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}4H\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}\in\llbracket h+1,H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\rho^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h+1}=s^{\prime})}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime\prime},a^{E})\biggr)

\displaystyle\leq\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\biggl(\sum\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(s,a)2Hb_{h}(s,a)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}\setminus\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(s,a)\Bigl(2Hb_{h}(s,a)+4H^{2}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},a^{E},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E})\Bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad+\max\limits_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}4H^{2}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},a^{E},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E})\biggr)

\displaystyle\leq\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\biggl(\sum\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(s,a)2Hb_{h}(s,a)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}\setminus\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(s,a)2Hb_{h}(s,a)+8H^{2}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},a^{E},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E})\biggr)

\displaystyle\leq 2H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(\cdot,\cdot)}b_{h}(s,a)+8H^{3}\max\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h}(s,a).

∎

F.3.2
Lemmas for Theorem
6.2

Lemma F.10

(Performance Decomposition Subset)
.

Under good event
\mathcal{E}
, it holds that:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\infty}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap},\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap})

\displaystyle\leq 2H^{2}\max\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}b_{h}(s,a)+4H^{3}\max\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h}(s,a).

Proof.

Observe that:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\infty}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap},\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap})

\displaystyle\coloneqq\max\{\sup\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}}\inf\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}}d_{\infty}(r,\widetilde{r}),\sup\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}}\inf\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}}d_{\infty}(r,\widetilde{r})\}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\sup\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}}\inf\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}}d_{\infty}(r,\widetilde{r})

\displaystyle\eqqcolon\sup\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}}\inf\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}}\frac{1}{M}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\max\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widetilde{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}\sup\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}}\frac{1}{M}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\max\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|,

(42)

where at (1) we have used that, under event
\mathcal{E}
, we have
\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}\subseteq\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}
,
and at (2) we have applied Lemma
F.6
, denoting with
\widehat{r}
the reward chosen from
\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}
.

By combining Eq.
F.3.2

with Eq.
F.3.1
,
Eq.
35
,
and Eq.
F.3.1
,
we get:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\infty}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap},\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap})

\displaystyle\leq\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\max\biggl\{\max\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}2Hb_{h}(s,a),

\displaystyle\qquad\max\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}\setminus\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}2Hb_{h}(s,a)+4H\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}\in\llbracket h+1,H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\rho_{h^{\prime}}^{p,\pi^{E}}(\cdot|s_{h+1}=s^{\prime})}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime\prime},a^{E}),

\displaystyle\qquad\max\limits_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}4H\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}\in\llbracket h+1,H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\rho^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h+1}=s^{\prime})}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime\prime},a^{E})\biggr\}

\displaystyle\leq\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\max\biggl\{\max\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}2Hb_{h}(s,a),

\displaystyle\qquad\max\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}\setminus\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}2Hb_{h}(s,a)+4H^{2}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},a^{E},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E}),

\displaystyle\qquad\max\limits_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}4H^{2}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},a^{E},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E})\biggr\}

\displaystyle\leq\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\max\biggl\{\max\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}2Hb_{h}(s,a),\max\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}\setminus\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}2Hb_{h}(s,a)+4H^{2}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},a^{E},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E})\biggr\}

\displaystyle\leq\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\max\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}2Hb_{h}(s,a)+\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}4H^{2}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},a^{E},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E})\biggr\}

\displaystyle\leq 2H^{2}\max\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}b_{h}(s,a)+4H^{3}\max\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h}(s,a).

∎

Lemma F.11

(Performance Decomposition Superset)
.

Under good event
\mathcal{E}
, it holds that:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\infty}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup})

\displaystyle\leq 2H^{2}\max\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}b_{h}(s,a)+4H^{3}\max\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h}(s,a).

Proof.

Observe that:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\infty}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup},\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup})

\displaystyle\coloneqq\max\{\sup\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}}\inf\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}}d_{\infty}(r,\widetilde{r}),\sup\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}}\inf\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}}d_{\infty}(r,\widetilde{r})\}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\sup\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}}\inf\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}}d_{\infty}(r,\widetilde{r})

\displaystyle\eqqcolon\sup\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}}\inf\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}}\frac{1}{M}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\max\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widetilde{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}\sup\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}}\frac{1}{M}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\max\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widetilde{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|,

(43)

where at (1) we have used that, under event
\mathcal{E}
, we have
\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}\subseteq\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}
,
and at (2) we have applied Lemma
F.7
, denoting with
r
the reward chosen from
\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}
.

By combining Eq.
F.3.2

with Eq.
38
,
Eq.
40
,
and Eq.
F.3.1
,
we get:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\infty}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup},\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup})

\displaystyle\leq\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\max\biggl\{\max\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}2Hb_{h}(s,a),

\displaystyle\qquad\max\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}\setminus\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}2Hb_{h}(s,a)+4H\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}\in\llbracket h+1,H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\rho_{h^{\prime}}^{p,\pi^{E}}(\cdot|s_{h+1}=s^{\prime})}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime\prime},a^{E}),

\displaystyle\qquad\max\limits_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}4H\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}\in\llbracket h+1,H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\rho^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h+1}=s^{\prime})}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime\prime},a^{E})\biggr\}

\displaystyle\leq\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\max\biggl\{\max\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}2Hb_{h}(s,a),

\displaystyle\qquad\max\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}\setminus\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}2Hb_{h}(s,a)+4H^{2}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},a^{E},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E}),

\displaystyle\qquad\max\limits_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}4H^{2}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},a^{E},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E})\biggr\}

\displaystyle\leq\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\max\biggl\{\max\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}2Hb_{h}(s,a),\max\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}\setminus\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h}}2Hb_{h}(s,a)+4H^{2}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},a^{E},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E})\biggr\}

\displaystyle\leq\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\max\limits_{(s,a)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}2Hb_{h}(s,a)+\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}4H^{2}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},a^{E},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E})\biggr\}

\displaystyle\leq 2H^{2}\max\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}b_{h}(s,a)+4H^{3}\max\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h}(s,a).

∎

F.3.3
Proofs of the main theorems

Thanks to Lemma
F.8

and Lemma
F.9
,
we can conclude the proof of Theorem
6.1
.
See
6.1

Proof.

The proof for the subset and superset is completely analogous.
Under good event
\mathcal{E}
, the performance decomposition
lemma for the subset (Lemma
F.8
) tells us that:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{d}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap},\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap})

\displaystyle\leq 2H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(\cdot,\cdot)}b_{h}(s,a)+8H^{3}\max\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h}(s,a)\leq\epsilon.

We upper bound both the terms of the sum by
\epsilon/2
.
The bound of the first term is analogous to the bound of the term provided in the proof of Theorem
5.1
, so we will not rewrite it here; with regards to the other term, we have:

\displaystyle 8H^{3}\max\limits_{(s,a^{E},h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h}(s,a^{E}))

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}8H^{3}\max\limits_{(s,a^{E},h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}\sqrt{2\frac{\beta(N_{h}^{b}(s,a^{E}),\delta)}{N_{h}^{b}(s,a^{E})}}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}8\sqrt{2}H^{3}\sqrt{\beta(\tau^{b},\delta)}\max\limits_{(s,a^{E},h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{h}^{b}(s,a^{E})}}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{\leq}8\sqrt{2}H^{3}\sqrt{\beta(\tau^{b},\delta)}\max\limits_{(s,a^{E},h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}\sqrt{c_{4}\frac{\ln\frac{|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}{\delta}}{\tau^{b}\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(s,a^{E})}}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(4)}}}{=}c_{5}H^{3}\sqrt{\frac{\beta(\tau^{b},\delta)\ln\frac{|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}{\delta}}{\tau^{b}\rho_{\min}^{\pi^{b},\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}}}\leq\epsilon/2,

where at (1) we have used the definition of the
b
terms,
at (2) we have upper bounded
\beta(N_{\bar{h}}^{b}(\bar{s},a^{E}),\delta)\leq\beta(\tau^{b},\delta)

for all
(\bar{s},\bar{h})\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}
, at (3) we use event
\mathcal{E}_{4}
,
at (4) we define
c_{5}\coloneqq 8\sqrt{2c_{4}}
and we use the definition of
\rho_{\min}^{\pi^{b},\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}
.

Similarly to the proof of Theorem
5.1
,
we apply Lemma
J.3
to (
c_{6}\coloneqq 4c_{5}^{2}
):

\displaystyle\tau^{b}\geq c_{6}\frac{H^{6}\ln\frac{|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}{\delta}}{\rho_{\min}^{\pi^{b},\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}\epsilon^{2}}\bigl(\ln\frac{4|\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}|}{\delta}+(|\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{\max}|-1)\ln\bigl(e(1+\tau^{b}/(|\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{\max}|-1))\bigr)\bigr),

to obtain:

\displaystyle\tau^{b}

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\Biggl(\frac{H^{6}\ln\frac{1}{\delta}}{\rho_{\min}^{\pi^{b},\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}\epsilon^{2}}\biggl(\ln\frac{1}{\delta}+|\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{\max}|\biggr)\Biggr).

We can do the same for the superset through Lemma
F.9
. By combining the various bounds, we get the result.
∎

Thanks to Lemma
F.10

and Lemma
F.11
,
we can conclude the proof of Theorem
6.2
.
See
6.2

Proof Sketch.

The proof is analogous to that of Theorem
6.1
.
The only difference is that we use Lemma
F.10
and Lemma
F.10
, and that
we follow the proof of Theorem
5.2
instead
of that of Theorem
5.1
to bound the first term of:

\displaystyle 2H^{2}\max\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}b_{h}(s,a)+4H^{3}\max\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h}(s,a)\leq\epsilon.

∎

F.4
Sample complexity for
PIRLO
with additional requirements

In the proofs of Theorem
6.1
and Theorem
6.2
, we have used reward choice lemmas that set
\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)\neq r_{h}(s,a)
in
(s,a,h)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}
. However, it might be interesting to relate directly the error in the estimation of the transition model with the difference in the reward functions, so that where we do not have samples we have zero error.
We would like to have
\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)=r_{h}(s,a)
in
(s,a,h)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}
. Notice that this property is satisfied in the proofs of Theorem
5.1

and Theorem
5.2
. Moreover, for a notion of distance other than
d
or
d_{\infty}
, the condition
\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)=r_{h}(s,a)
in
(s,a,h)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}
might even be needed.
Therefore, in this section, we provide reward choice lemmas that satisfy this property, and we show that this selection ends up in a
H^{8}
dependence in the sample complexity instead of
H^{6}
.

Lemma F.12

(Reward Choice Subset)
.

Under good event
\mathcal{E}
, for any
r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}
,
the reward
\widehat{r}
constructed (recursively) as:

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a^{E})=r_{h}(s,a^{E})+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r)-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\\
\qquad\qquad\qquad+\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r),\qquad\forall(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}\\
\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)=r_{h}(s,a),\quad\forall(s,a,h)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}\\
\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)=r_{h}(s,a)+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p^{M}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r}),\quad\text{otherwise}\\
\end{cases},

belongs to
\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}
.

Proof.

By definition of
\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}
, the reward
\widehat{r}
belongs to
\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}

if and only if:

\displaystyle\forall(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}},\forall a\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\{a^{E}\}:Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\geq Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h}(s,a;\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r}).

By hypothesis,
r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}
, therefore:

\displaystyle\forall(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}},\forall a\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\{a^{E}\}:Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r)\geq Q^{\pi^{M}}_{h}(s,a;p^{M},r),

thus, if we show that
\forall(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}},\forall a\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\{a^{E}\}
,
it holds that:

\displaystyle Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h}(s,a;\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\geq Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r)-Q^{\pi^{M}}_{h}(s,a;p^{M},r),

then we are done.

Let us begin with triples
(s,a,h)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}
such that
(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}
. By rearranging the terms in the definition of
\widehat{r}
, we observe that:

\displaystyle\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a^{E})+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})=r_{h}(s,a^{E})+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)

\displaystyle\iff Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})=Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r)+\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{\iff}Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})=Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r)+\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)+\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-\bigl({\color[rgb]{0,0,1}r_{h}(s,a)}+\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)\bigr)

\displaystyle\iff Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})=Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r)+Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h}(s,a;\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-Q^{\pi^{M}}_{h}(s,a;p^{M},r)

\displaystyle\implies Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h}(s,a;\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\geq Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r)-Q^{\pi^{M}}_{h}(s,a;p^{M},r),

where at (1) we have used that
\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)=r_{h}(s,a)
by definition.

Now, consider any other triple
(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}\setminus\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}
such that

(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}
. By rearranging the terms,
we obtain:

\displaystyle Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h}(s,a;\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})=Q^{\pi^{M}}_{h}(s,a;p^{M},r),

(44)

therefore, it suffices to show that

\displaystyle Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\geq Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r).

By using again the definition of
\widehat{r}
for
(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}
, we know that:

\displaystyle Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})=Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r)+\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r),

(45)

therefore, if we show that

\displaystyle\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\geq\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r),

then we are done. We do it by induction. At stage
H-1
,
we have that:

\displaystyle\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{H}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})

\displaystyle=\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\E\limits_{a^{\prime}\sim\widetilde{\pi}^{M}_{H}(\cdot|s^{\prime})}\widehat{r}_{H}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\E\limits_{a^{\prime}\sim\pi^{M}_{H}(\cdot|s^{\prime})}r_{H}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{M}}_{H}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r),

where at (1) we have used the definition of
\widehat{r}
at stage
H
, and the definitions of
\widetilde{\pi}^{M}
and
\pi^{M}
.
We make the inductive hypothesis that, at stage
h+1
, it holds that

\max_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\geq\max_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)
, and we consider stage
h
:

\displaystyle\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\max\Bigl\{\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}Q^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pi^{E}}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r}),\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\Bigr\}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\geq}\max\Bigl\{\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\widetilde{p}^{m}},\widehat{r}),\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\Bigr\}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{\geq}\max\Bigl\{\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}p},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}r}),\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\Bigr\}

\displaystyle=\max\Bigl\{\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r),

\displaystyle\qquad\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\max\bigl\{\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r}),\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr\}\Bigr\}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(4)}}}{=}\max\Bigl\{\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r),

\displaystyle\qquad\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\max\bigl\{\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}Q^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pi^{M}}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}p^{M}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}r}),

\displaystyle\qquad\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}\widehat{r}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})+\max\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr\}\Bigr\}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(5)}}}{\geq}\max\Bigl\{\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r),

\displaystyle\qquad\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\max\bigl\{\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}Q^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};p^{M},r),

\displaystyle\qquad\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}r}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})+\max\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}p^{M}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}r})\bigr\}\Bigr\}

\displaystyle=\max\Bigl\{\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r),

\displaystyle\qquad\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\max\bigl\{\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}Q^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};p^{M},r),\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}Q^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};p^{M},r)\bigr\}\Bigr\}

\displaystyle=\max\Bigl\{\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r),\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};p^{M},r)\Bigr\}

\displaystyle=\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r),

where at (1) we use the definition of
\widetilde{\pi}^{M}
, at (2) we use the definition of
\widetilde{p}^{m}
and
\widetilde{p}^{M}
,
at (3) we use the inductive hypothesis along with Eq.
45
,
at (4) we use Eq.
44
and the definition of Q-function,
at (5) we use the definition of
\widehat{r}
and the inductive hypothesis.

This concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma F.13

(Reward Choice Superset)
.

Under good event
\mathcal{E}
, for any
\widehat{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}
,
the reward
r
constructed (recursively) as:

\displaystyle\begin{cases}r_{h}(s,a^{E})=\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a^{E})+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r)\\
\qquad\qquad\qquad+\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r)-\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r}),\qquad\forall(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}\\
r_{h}(s,a)=\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a),\quad\forall(s,a,h)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}\\
r_{h}(s,a)=\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p^{m}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a)V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r),\quad\text{otherwise}\\
\end{cases},

belongs to
\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}
.

Proof.

By definition of
\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}
, a sufficient condition for having the reward
r
belong to
\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}
is:

\displaystyle\forall(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}},\forall a\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\{a^{E}\}:Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r)\geq Q^{\pi^{m}}_{h}(s,a;p^{m},r).

By hypothesis,
r\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}
, therefore:

\displaystyle\forall(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}},\forall a\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\{a^{E}\}:Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\geq Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h}(s,a;\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r}),

thus, if we show that
\forall(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}},\forall a\in\mathcal{A}\setminus\{a^{E}\}
,
it holds that:

\displaystyle Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r)-Q^{\pi^{m}}_{h}(s,a;p^{m},r)\geq Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h}(s,a;\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r}),

then we are done.

Let us begin with triples
(s,a,h)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}
such that
(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}
. By rearranging the terms in the definition of
r
, we observe that:

\displaystyle\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a^{E})+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})=r_{h}(s,a^{E})+\sum\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}p_{h}(s^{\prime}|s,a^{E})V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r)

\displaystyle\iff Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})=Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r)+\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r){\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pm r_{h}(s,a)}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{\iff}Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})=Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r)+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)}+\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-\bigl(r_{h}(s,a)+\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r)\bigr)

\displaystyle\iff Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})=Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r)+Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h}(s,a;\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-Q^{\pi^{m}}_{h}(s,a;p^{m},r)

\displaystyle\implies Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h}(s,a;\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\geq Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r)-Q^{\pi^{m}}_{h}(s,a;p^{m},r),

where at (1) we have used that
r_{h}(s,a)=\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)
by definition.

Now, consider any other triple
(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}\setminus\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}
such that

(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}
. By rearranging the terms,
we obtain:

\displaystyle Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h}(s,a;\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})=Q^{\pi^{m}}_{h}(s,a;p^{m},r),

(46)

therefore, it suffices to show that

\displaystyle Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r)\geq Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r}).

By using again the definition of
\widehat{r}
for
(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}
, we know that:

\displaystyle Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})=Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h}(s,a^{E};p,r)+\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r),

(47)

therefore, if we show that

\displaystyle\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\leq\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r),

then we are done. We do it by induction. At stage
H-1
,
we have that:

\displaystyle\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{H}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})

\displaystyle=\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\E\limits_{a^{\prime}\sim\widetilde{\pi}^{m}_{H}(\cdot|s^{\prime})}\widehat{r}_{H}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\E\limits_{a^{\prime}\sim\pi^{m}_{H}(\cdot|s^{\prime})}r_{H}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{m}}_{H}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r),

where at (1) we have used the definition of
\widehat{r}
at stage
H
, and also the definitions of
\widetilde{\pi}^{m}
and
\pi^{m}
.
We make the inductive hypothesis that, at stage
h+1
, it holds that

\min_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\leq\min_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r)
, and we consider stage
h
:

\displaystyle\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\min\Bigl\{\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}Q^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pi^{E}}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r}),\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\Bigr\}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}\min\Bigl\{\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\widetilde{p}^{M}},\widehat{r}),\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\Bigr\}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{\leq}\min\Bigl\{\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}p},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}r}),\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\Bigr\}

\displaystyle=\min\Bigl\{\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r),

\displaystyle\qquad\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\max\bigl\{\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r}),\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr\}\Bigr\}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(4)}}}{=}\min\Bigl\{\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r),

\displaystyle\qquad\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\max\bigl\{\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}Q^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pi^{m}}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}p^{m}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}r}),

\displaystyle\qquad\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}\widehat{r}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})+\min\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr\}\Bigr\}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(5)}}}{\leq}\min\Bigl\{\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r),

\displaystyle\qquad\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\max\bigl\{\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}Q^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};p^{m},r),
