\displaystyle\qquad\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}r}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})+\max\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}p^{m}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}r})\bigr\}\Bigr\}

\displaystyle=\min\Bigl\{\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r),

\displaystyle\qquad\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\max\bigl\{\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}Q^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};p^{m},r),\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}Q^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};p^{m},r)\bigr\}\Bigr\}

\displaystyle=\min\Bigl\{\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r),\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};p^{m},r)\Bigr\}

\displaystyle=\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r),

where at (1) we use the definition of
\widetilde{\pi}^{m}
, at (2) we use the definition of
\widetilde{p}^{m}
and
\widetilde{p}^{M}
,
at (3) we use the inductive hypothesis along with Eq.
47
,
at (4) we use Eq.
46
and the Bellman’s equation,
at (5) we use the definition of
r
and the inductive hypothesis.

This concludes the proof.
∎

F.4.1
Lemmas for Theorem
F.18

We can exploit Lemma
F.12
to bound the error for the subset.

Lemma F.14

(Performance Decomposition Subset)
.

Under good event
\mathcal{E}
, it holds that:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{d}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap},\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap})

\displaystyle\leq 2H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(\cdot,\cdot)}b_{h}(s,a)+4H^{4}\max\limits_{(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h}(s,a^{E}).

Proof.

We can write:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{H}_{d}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap},\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap})&\coloneqq\max\{\sup\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}}\inf\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}}d(r,\widetilde{r}),\sup\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}}\inf\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}}d(r,\widetilde{r})\}\\
&\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\sup\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}}\inf\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}}d(r,\widetilde{r})\\
&\eqqcolon\sup\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}}\inf\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}}\frac{1}{M}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\biggl(\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(\cdot,\cdot)}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widetilde{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|+\max\limits_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widetilde{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|\biggr)\\
&\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}\sup\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}}\frac{1}{M}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\biggl(\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(\cdot,\cdot)}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|+\underbrace{\max\limits_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|}_{=0}\biggr)\\
&=\sup\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}}\frac{1}{M}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(\cdot,\cdot)}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|\end{split}

(48)

where at (1) we have used that, under good event
\mathcal{E}
,
\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}\subseteq\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}
,
and at (2) we apply Lemma
F.12
, by denoting with
\widehat{r}
the chosen reward from
\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap}
.

In the following, it is useful to denote, for any
h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket
:

\displaystyle X_{h}\coloneqq\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\bigl|V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)\bigr|.

Let us consider any
(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}
. The difference between the rewards of expert’s action can be bounded by:

\displaystyle\bigl|\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a^{E})-r_{h}(s,a^{E})\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{\leq}\bigl|\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r)-\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigl|\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}\bigl|\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r)-\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\pm\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}}\bigl|V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{\leq}MH\bigl|\bigl|p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})-\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})\bigr|\bigr|_{1}+\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})}\bigl|V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r)-V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|+X_{h}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(4)}}}{\leq}2MHb_{h}(s,a^{E})+\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})}\bigl|Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r)-Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|+X_{h}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(5)}}}{=}2MHb_{h}(s,a^{E})+\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})}\bigl|\max\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};p^{M},r)-\max\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr|+X_{h}

\displaystyle\leq 2MHb_{h}(s,a^{E})+\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})}\bigl[X_{h+1}\bigr]+X_{h}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(6)}}}{=}2MHb_{h}(s,a^{E})+X_{h}+X_{h+1},

where at (1) we use the definition of
\widehat{r}
in Lemma
F.12
and triangle inequality, at (2) we use that, for any pair
f,g
of real-valued functions, it holds that
|\max_{x}f(x)-\max_{x}g(x)|\leq\max_{x}|f(x)-g(x)|
, at (3) we apply triangle inequality twice, we recognize the definition of
X_{h}
, we upper bound the value function by
MH
, and we recognize the definition of
\ell_{1}
norm,
at (4) we first use triangle inequality
\|p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})-\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})\|_{1}\leq\|p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})-\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})\|_{1}+\|\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})-\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})\|_{1}
, then we use Pinsker’s inequality, event
\mathcal{E}_{3}
from Lemma
F.2
, and the definition of
b_{h}(s,a^{E})
;
at (5) we use the definition of
\widehat{r}
in Lemma
F.12
in the form of Eq.
45
, by noticing that the support of
p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})

is contained in
\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}
, and at (6) we realize that
X_{h+1}
depends
only on
h
and not on
s^{\prime}
.

In order to upper bound the term
X_{h}
, we write:

\displaystyle X_{h}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\bigl|V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\max\biggl\{\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\Bigl|\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};p^{M},r)\Bigr|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\Bigl|Q^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pi^{E}}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-Q^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pi^{E}}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}p},r)\Bigr|\biggr\}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}\max\biggl\{\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}}\Bigl|Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-Q^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};p^{M},r)\Bigr|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\Bigl|Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r)\pm Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\Bigr|\biggr\}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{=}\max\biggl\{\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\max\Bigl\{\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}\Bigl|\underbrace{Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-Q^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};p^{M},r)}_{=0}\Bigr|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}\Bigl|Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-Q^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};p^{M},r)\Bigr|\Bigr\},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\Bigl|Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})+\max\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-\max\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)\Bigr|\biggr\}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(4)}}}{\leq}\max\biggl\{\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}\Bigl|\underbrace{\widehat{r}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})-r_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})}_{=0}+\max\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-\max\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};p^{M},r)\Bigr|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\Bigl|Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\Bigr|+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}X_{h+1}}\biggr\}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(5)}}}{\leq}\max\biggl\{X_{h+1},\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\Bigl(\Bigl|\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h+1}(\cdot|s^{\prime},a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h+1}(\cdot|s^{\prime},a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\Bigr|+X_{h+1}\Bigr)\biggr\}

\displaystyle=X_{h+1}+\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\Bigl|\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h+1}(\cdot|s^{\prime},a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h+1}(\cdot|s^{\prime},a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\pm\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h+1}(\cdot|s^{\prime},a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\Bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(6)}}}{\leq}X_{h+1}+\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\biggl(MH\Bigl\|\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h+1}(\cdot|s^{\prime},a^{E})-\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h+1}(\cdot|s^{\prime},a^{E})\Bigr\|_{1}+\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h+1}(\cdot|s^{\prime},a^{E})}\Bigl|V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\Bigr|\biggr)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(7)}}}{\leq}X_{h+1}+\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\biggl(2MHb_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h+1}(\cdot|s^{\prime},a^{E})}\Bigl|\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h+2}(\cdot|s^{\prime\prime},a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+3}(s^{\prime\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h+2}(\cdot|s^{\prime\prime},a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+3}(s^{\prime\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\Bigr|\biggr)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(8)}}}{\leq}X_{h+1}+2MH\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}\in\llbracket h+1,H-1\rrbracket}\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\rho^{\widetilde{p}^{m},\pi^{E}}_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h+1}=s^{\prime})}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime\prime},a^{E})

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(9)}}}{\leq}2MH\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}\in\llbracket h,H-1\rrbracket}\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h^{\prime}+1}}\sum\limits_{h^{\prime\prime}\in\llbracket h^{\prime}+1,H-1\rrbracket}\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\rho^{\widetilde{p}^{m},\pi^{E}}_{h^{\prime\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h^{\prime}+1}=s^{\prime})}b_{h^{\prime\prime}}(s^{\prime\prime},a^{E})

\displaystyle\leq 2MH^{3}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E}),

where at (1) we apply the Bellman’s equation and the definition of
\widetilde{\pi}^{M}
and
\pi^{M}
, at (2) we use that

|\max_{x}f(x)-\max_{x}g(x)|\leq\max_{x}|f(x)-g(x)|
,
at (3) we use the definition of
\widehat{r}
in the form of Eq.

44
and Eq.
45
, at (4) we apply the Bellman’s equation and the definition of
\widehat{r}
to recognize that
\widehat{r}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})-r_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})=0
; moreover, we apply triangle inequality along with the usual bound

|\max_{x}f(x)-\max_{x}g(x)|\leq\max_{x}|f(x)-g(x)|
, and we recognize the definition of
X_{h+1}
. At (5) we proceed similarly as (4) and we use the Bellman optimality equation, and we observe that
X_{h+1}
does not depend on

s^{\prime}
; at (6) we upper bound the value function by
HM
and recognize the
\ell_{1}
-norm, at (7) we use the concentration bound of event
\mathcal{E}_{3}
in Lemma
F.2
(both
\widetilde{p}^{M}
and
\widetilde{p}^{m}
lie at a “distance” of
b
from
\widehat{p}
). At (8) we have unfolded the recursion to bound the difference of value functions between transition models

\widetilde{p}^{m}
and
\widetilde{p}^{M}
, at (9) we have unfolded the recursion on the
X
terms.

Thanks to this expression, we can upper bound the difference of rewards in expert’s action as:

\displaystyle\bigl|\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a^{E})-r_{h}(s,a^{E})\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq 2MHb_{h}(s,a^{E})+4MH^{3}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E}).

With regards to visited
(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}\setminus\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}
, we can write:

\displaystyle\bigl|\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)-r_{h}(s,a)\bigr|

\displaystyle=\bigl|\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq 2MHb_{h}(s,a)+\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}\Bigl|V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)\Bigr|

\displaystyle\leq 2MHb_{h}(s,a)+\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Bigl|V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-V^{\pi^{M}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{M},r)\Bigr|

\displaystyle=2MHb_{h}(s,a)+X_{h}

\displaystyle\leq 2MHb_{h}(s,a)+2MH^{3}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E})

\displaystyle\leq 2MHb_{h}(s,a)+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}4}MH^{3}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E}).

Obviously, for
(s,a,h)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}
, we have:

\displaystyle\bigl|\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)-r_{h}(s,a)\bigr|

\displaystyle=0.

Therefore, by Eq.
48
, we can write:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{d}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap},\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap})

\displaystyle\leq\sup\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap}}\frac{1}{M}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(\cdot,\cdot)}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(\cdot,\cdot)}\biggl(2Hb_{h}(s,a)+4H^{3}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E})\biggr)

\displaystyle=2H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(\cdot,\cdot)}b_{h}(s,a)+4H^{3}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(\cdot,\cdot)}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E})

\displaystyle=2H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(\cdot,\cdot)}b_{h}(s,a)+4H^{3}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E})

\displaystyle=2H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(\cdot,\cdot)}b_{h}(s,a)+4H^{4}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E}).

This concludes the proof w.r.t. the subset.
∎

Now we can exploit Lemma
F.13
to bound the error for the superset.

Lemma F.15

(Performance Decomposition Superset)
.

Under good event
\mathcal{E}
, it holds that:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{d}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup},\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup})

\displaystyle\leq 2H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}(\cdot,\cdot)}b_{h}(s,a)+4H^{4}\max\limits_{(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h}(s,a^{E}).

Proof.

We can write:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{H}_{d}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup},\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup})&\coloneqq\max\{\sup\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}}\inf\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}}d(r,\widetilde{r}),\sup\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}}\inf\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}}d(r,\widetilde{r})\}\\
&\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\sup\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}}\inf\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}}d(r,\widetilde{r})\\
&\eqqcolon\sup\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}}\inf\limits_{r\in\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}}\frac{1}{M}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\biggl(\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(\cdot,\cdot)}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widetilde{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|+\max\limits_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widetilde{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|\biggr)\\
&\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}\sup\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}}\frac{1}{M}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\biggl(\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(\cdot,\cdot)}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widetilde{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|+\underbrace{\max\limits_{(s,a)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}_{h}}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widetilde{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|}_{=0}\biggr)\\
&=\sup\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}}\frac{1}{M}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(\cdot,\cdot)}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widetilde{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|\end{split}

(49)

where at (1) we have used that, under good event
\mathcal{E}
,
\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}\subseteq\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}
,
and at (2) we apply Lemma
F.13
, by denoting with
r
the chosen reward from
\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup}
.

In the following, it is useful to denote, for any
h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket
:

\displaystyle Y_{h}\coloneqq\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\bigl|V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r)\bigr|.

Let us consider any
(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}
. The difference between the rewards of expert’s action can be bounded by:

\displaystyle\bigl|\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a^{E})-r_{h}(s,a^{E})\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{\leq}\bigl|\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r)-\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigl|\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-\min\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r)\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}\bigl|\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r)-\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\pm\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr|

\displaystyle\qquad+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}}\bigl|V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r)\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{\leq}MH\bigl|\bigl|p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})-\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})\bigr|\bigr|_{1}+\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})}\bigl|V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p,r)-V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr|+Y_{h}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(4)}}}{\leq}2MHb_{h}(s,a^{E})+\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})}\bigl|Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r)-Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\bigr|+Y_{h}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(5)}}}{=}2MHb_{h}(s,a^{E})+\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})}\bigl|\min\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};p^{m},r)-\min\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\bigr|+Y_{h}

\displaystyle\leq 2MHb_{h}(s,a^{E})+\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})}\bigl[Y_{h+1}\bigr]+Y_{h}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(6)}}}{=}2MHb_{h}(s,a^{E})+Y_{h}+Y_{h+1},

where at (1) we use the definition of
r
in Lemma
F.13
and triangle inequality, at (2) we use that, for any pair
f,g
of real-valued functions, it holds that
|\min_{x}f(x)-\min_{x}g(x)|\leq\max_{x}|f(x)-g(x)|
, at (3) we apply triangle inequality twice, we recognize the definition of
X_{h}
, we upper bound the value function by
MH
, and we recognize the definition of
\ell_{1}
norm,
at (4) we first use triangle inequality
\|p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})-\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})\|_{1}\leq\|p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})-\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})\|_{1}+\|\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})-\widehat{p}_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})\|_{1}
, then we use Pinsker’s inequality, event
\mathcal{E}_{3}
from Lemma
F.2
, and the definition of
b_{h}(s,a^{E})
;
at (5) we use the definition of
\widehat{r}
in Lemma
F.13
in the form of Eq.
47
, by noticing that the support of
p_{h}(\cdot|s,a^{E})

is contained in
\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}
, and at (6) we realize that
Y_{h+1}
depends
only on
h
and not on
s^{\prime}
.

In order to upper bound the term
Y_{h}
, we write:

\displaystyle Y_{h}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\bigl|V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r)\bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(1)}}}{=}\max\biggl\{\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\Bigl|\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}Q^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};p^{m},r)\Bigr|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\Bigl|Q^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pi^{E}}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-Q^{{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\pi^{E}}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}p},r)\Bigr|\biggr\}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(2)}}}{\leq}\max\biggl\{\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}}}\Bigl|Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-Q^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};p^{m},r)\Bigr|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\Bigl|Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};p,r)\pm Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\Bigr|\biggr\}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(3)}}}{=}\max\biggl\{\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\max\Bigl\{\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}\Bigl|\underbrace{Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-Q^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};p^{m},r)}_{=0}\Bigr|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}\Bigl|Q^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-Q^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime};p^{m},r)\Bigr|\Bigr\},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\Bigl|Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})+\min\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-\min\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r)\Bigr|\biggr\}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(4)}}}{\leq}\max\biggl\{\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\notin\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\max\limits_{a^{\prime}\in\mathcal{A}:(s^{\prime},a^{\prime},h+1)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}\Bigl|\underbrace{\widehat{r}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})-r_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})}_{=0}+\min\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-\min\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};p^{m},r)\Bigr|,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\Bigl|Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-Q^{\pi^{E}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\Bigr|+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}Y_{h+1}}\biggr\}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(5)}}}{\leq}\max\biggl\{Y_{h+1},\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\Bigl(\Bigl|\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h+1}(\cdot|s^{\prime},a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h+1}(\cdot|s^{\prime},a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\Bigr|+Y_{h+1}\Bigr)\biggr\}

\displaystyle=Y_{h+1}+\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\Bigl|\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h+1}(\cdot|s^{\prime},a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h+1}(\cdot|s^{\prime},a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\pm\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h+1}(\cdot|s^{\prime},a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})\Bigr|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(6)}}}{\leq}Y_{h+1}+\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\biggl(MH\Bigl\|\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h+1}(\cdot|s^{\prime},a^{E})-\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h+1}(\cdot|s^{\prime},a^{E})\Bigr\|_{1}+\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h+1}(\cdot|s^{\prime},a^{E})}\Bigl|V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+2}(s^{\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\Bigr|\biggr)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(7)}}}{\leq}Y_{h+1}+\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\biggl(2MHb_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{E})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h+1}(\cdot|s^{\prime},a^{E})}\Bigl|\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{M}_{h+2}(\cdot|s^{\prime\prime},a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+3}(s^{\prime\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{M},\widehat{r})-\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h+2}(\cdot|s^{\prime\prime},a^{E})}V^{\pi^{E}}_{h+3}(s^{\prime\prime\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})\Bigr|\biggr)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(8)}}}{\leq}Y_{h+1}+2MH\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h+1}}\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}\in\llbracket h+1,H-1\rrbracket}\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\rho^{\widetilde{p}^{m},\pi^{E}}_{h^{\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h+1}=s^{\prime})}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime\prime},a^{E})

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{\makebox[0.0pt]{(9)}}}{\leq}2MH\sum\limits_{h^{\prime}\in\llbracket h,H-1\rrbracket}\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}_{h^{\prime}+1}}\sum\limits_{h^{\prime\prime}\in\llbracket h^{\prime}+1,H-1\rrbracket}\E\limits_{s^{\prime\prime}\sim\rho^{\widetilde{p}^{m},\pi^{E}}_{h^{\prime\prime}}(\cdot|s_{h^{\prime}+1}=s^{\prime})}b_{h^{\prime\prime}}(s^{\prime\prime},a^{E})

\displaystyle\leq 2MH^{3}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E}),

where at (1) we apply the Bellman’s equation and the definition of
\widetilde{\pi}^{m}
and
\pi^{m}
, at (2) we use that

|\min_{x}f(x)-\min_{x}g(x)|\leq\max_{x}|f(x)-g(x)|
,
at (3) we use the definition of
r
in the form of Eq.

46
and Eq.
47
, at (4) we apply the Bellman’s equation and the definition of
r
to recognize that
r_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})-\widehat{r}_{h+1}(s^{\prime},a^{\prime})=0
; moreover, we apply triangle inequality along with the usual bound

|\min_{x}f(x)-\min_{x}g(x)|\leq\max_{x}|f(x)-g(x)|
, and we recognize the definition of
X_{h+1}
. At (5) we proceed similarly as (4) and we use the Bellman’s equation, and we observe that
Y_{h+1}
does not depend on

s^{\prime}
; at (6) we upper bound the value function by
HM
and recognize the
\ell_{1}
-norm, at (7) we use the concentration bound of event
\mathcal{E}_{3}
in Lemma
F.2
(both
\widetilde{p}^{M}
and
\widetilde{p}^{m}
lie at a “distance” of
b
from
\widehat{p}
). At (8) we have unfolded the recursion to bound the difference of value functions between transition models

\widetilde{p}^{m}
and
\widetilde{p}^{M}
, at (9) we have unfolded the recursion on the
Y
terms.

Thanks to this expression, we can upper bound the difference of rewards in expert’s action as:

\displaystyle\bigl|\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a^{E})-r_{h}(s,a^{E})\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq 2MHb_{h}(s,a^{E})+4MH^{3}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E}).

With regards to visited
(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}\setminus\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}
, we can write:

\displaystyle\bigl|\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)-r_{h}(s,a)\bigr|

\displaystyle=\bigl|\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim p_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r)\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq 2MHb_{h}(s,a)+\E\limits_{s^{\prime}\sim\widetilde{p}^{m}_{h}(\cdot|s,a)}\Bigl|V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r)\Bigr|

\displaystyle\leq 2MHb_{h}(s,a)+\max\limits_{s^{\prime}\in\mathcal{S}}\Bigl|V^{\widetilde{\pi}^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};\widetilde{p}^{m},\widehat{r})-V^{\pi^{m}}_{h+1}(s^{\prime};p^{m},r)\Bigr|

\displaystyle=2MHb_{h}(s,a)+Y_{h}

\displaystyle\leq 2MHb_{h}(s,a)+2MH^{3}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E})

\displaystyle\leq 2MHb_{h}(s,a)+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}4}MH^{3}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E}).

Obviously, for
(s,a,h)\notin\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}
, we have:

\displaystyle\bigl|\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)-r_{h}(s,a)\bigr|

\displaystyle=0.

Therefore, by Eq.
49
, we can write:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{d}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cup},\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup})

\displaystyle\leq\sup\limits_{\widetilde{r}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cup}}\frac{1}{M}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(\cdot,\cdot)}\bigl|r_{h}(s,a)-\widehat{r}_{h}(s,a)\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(\cdot,\cdot)}\biggl(2Hb_{h}(s,a)+4H^{3}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E})\biggr)

\displaystyle=2H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(\cdot,\cdot)}b_{h}(s,a)+4H^{3}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(\cdot,\cdot)}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E})

\displaystyle=2H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(\cdot,\cdot)}b_{h}(s,a)+4H^{3}\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E})

\displaystyle=2H\sum\limits_{h\in\llbracket H\rrbracket}\E\limits_{(s,a)\sim\rho_{h}^{p,\pi^{b}}(\cdot,\cdot)}b_{h}(s,a)+4H^{4}\max\limits_{(s^{\prime},h^{\prime})\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h^{\prime}}(s^{\prime},a^{E}).

This concludes the proof w.r.t. the superset.
∎

F.4.2
Lemmas for Theorem
F.19

Lemma F.16

(Performance Decomposition Subset)
.

Under good event
\mathcal{E}
, it holds that:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\infty}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap},\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap})

\displaystyle\leq 2H^{2}\max\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}b_{h}(s,a)+4H^{4}\max\limits_{(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h}(s,a^{E}).

Proof Sketch.

The proof is analogous to that of Lemma
F.14
.
We can reuse the bounds for the difference between rewards proved in there and insert them into
\mathcal{H}_{\infty}
to get the result.
∎

Lemma F.17

(Performance Decomposition Superset)
.

Under good event
\mathcal{E}
, it holds that:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\infty}(\mathcal{R}_{p,\pi^{E}}^{\cap},\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}^{\cap})

\displaystyle\leq 2H^{2}\max\limits_{(s,a,h)\in\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}b_{h}(s,a)+4H^{4}\max\limits_{(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}^{p,\pi^{E}}}b_{h}(s,a^{E}).

Proof Sketch.

The proof is analogous to that of Lemma
F.15
.
We can reuse the bounds for the difference between rewards proved in there and insert them into
\mathcal{H}_{\infty}
to get the result.
∎

F.4.3
Proofs of the main theorems

Thanks to Lemma
F.14
and Lemma
F.15
,
we can conclude the proof of the main theorem for
d
.

Theorem F.18
.

Under the conditions of Theorem
6.1
,
PIRLO
is
(\epsilon,\delta)
-PAC for
d
-IRL
with a sample complexity at most:

\displaystyle\tau^{b}

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\Biggl(\frac{H^{3}Z^{p,\pi^{b}}\ln\frac{1}{\delta}}{\epsilon^{2}}\biggl(\ln\frac{1}{\delta}+S_{\max}^{p,\pi^{b}}\biggr)

\displaystyle+\frac{H^{8}\ln\frac{1}{\delta}}{\rho_{\min}^{\pi^{b},\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}\epsilon^{2}}\biggl(\ln\frac{1}{\delta}+S_{\max}^{p,\pi^{b}}\biggr)+\frac{\ln\frac{1}{\delta}}{\ln\frac{1}{1-\rho^{\pi^{b},\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}_{\min}}}\Biggr),

and
\tau^{E}
is bounded as in Theorem
5.1
. Furthermore,
PIRLO
is inclusion monotonic.

Proof Sketch.

The proof for the subset and superset is completely analogous.
Thanks to Lemma
F.14
and Lemma
F.15
, we realize that we have to bound the sum of two terms, which are completely analogous to those in the proof of Theorem
6.1
, with the only difference of
H^{4}
instead of
H^{3}
. By proceeding similarly, we get the result.
∎

F.16
and Lemma
F.17
,
we can conclude the proof of the main theorem for

.

.

6.1
,

PIRLO
is
(\epsilon,\delta)
-PAC for
d_{\infty}
-IRL
with a sample complexity at most:

\displaystyle\tau^{b}

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\Bigg(\frac{H^{4}\ln\frac{1}{\delta}}{\rho^{\pi^{b},\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}_{\min}\epsilon^{2}}\biggl(\ln\frac{1}{\delta}+S^{p,\pi^{b}}_{\max}\biggr)

\displaystyle+\frac{H^{8}\ln\frac{1}{\delta}}{\rho_{\min}^{\pi^{b},\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{E}}}\epsilon^{2}}\biggl(\ln\frac{1}{\delta}+S^{p,\pi^{b}}_{\max}\biggr)+\frac{\ln\frac{1}{\delta}}{\ln\frac{1}{1-\rho^{\pi^{b},\mathcal{Z}^{p,\pi^{b}}}_{\min}}}\Bigg),

and
\tau^{E}
is bounded as in Theorem
5.1
.
Furthermore,
PIRLO
is inclusion monotonic.

Proof Sketch.

Thanks to Lemma
F.16
and Lemma
F.17
, we realize that we have to bound the sum of two terms, which are completely analogous to those in the proof of Theorem
6.2
, with the only difference of
H^{4}
instead of

. By proceeding similarly, we get the result.
